## Best date plans

### 2026-10-03 — The third united lenape / lunáapeew nations pow wow + Rangoon

**The third united lenape / lunáapeew nations pow wow, then a meal.** Burmese at Rangoon. It is 1.3 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=171+East+Dr%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&destination=500+Prospect+Pl%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/third-united-lenape-lunaapeew-nations-pow-wow-tickets-1998442834314) · [Restaurant](https://www.rangoon.nyc/)

Open-air night market behind the New York Hall of Science with about a hundred independent food, art, and merchandise vendors, deliberately kept low-priced.

5:00 PM · museums · Free · Whitney Museum of American Art · Meatpacking District · Manhattan · Score 54.0  
The Whitney is free to all every Friday evening from 5 to 10pm, and also on the second Sunday of each month.
**Timing:** Free admission Fridays 5-10pm. Free tickets are limited and released in advance.
**Practical note:** Reserve the free timed ticket ahead; walk-up availability runs out on busy evenings.
**Why now:** A standing free window that makes a good anchor for a Friday evening in the Meatpacking District.

## Where to eat

Verified 2026-10-03 dining picks with corrections for duplicates, current dates, prices, award wording, and menu classification.

Dining research: 2026-10-03T10:33:15+00:00

No candidate is verified here as newly holding a Michelin star. Michelin references in checked sources are background only: Fonn cites a chef from Michelin-starred Restaurant Daniel; Humm references Daniel Humm/Eleven Madison Park history; Eater mentions prior Michelin-starred Piora in an unrelated background item. Crown Shy was checked as an official menu source only; no award claim should be added.

### All-you-can-eat Southern buffet dinner at Good Time Country Buffet

Deals & happy hours — Good Time Country Buffet

Useful affordable Manhattan dinner: the Kisa/C as in Charlie team opened a Southern-style all-you-can-eat buffet with fried chicken, collards, mac and cheese, rotating specials, and separate desserts/drinks.

Price: $26.99 adults; $23.99 age 65+; $13.99 kids age 4-9; desserts and drinks extra  
Schedule: Wednesday-Sunday, 5:00 PM-10:00 PM  
Conditions: Walk-ins plus limited Resy reservations reported; drinks and desserts cost extra; takeout not offered at launch per Resy; tax/tip/service not stated.  
Opened: 2026-09-12  
Published: 2026-09-09  
[Source](https://blog.resy.com/2026/09/good-time-country-buffet-nyc/)

### Weekday happy hour at Rosemary's West Village

Deals & happy hours — Rosemary's West Village

Official menu lists a weekday happy hour with $7 wines, $12 cocktails, $6 draft lager, and aperitivo snacks, making it a useful central-Manhattan deal before dinner.

Price: $7 wines; $12 cocktails; $6 Forst Lager draft; snacks $9-$21
